Alternative: Use a dedicated "active set dual" algorithm from previous SOTA: They solve LP maximizing sum g subject to f*g≤M, Frank-Wolfe. Maybe can improve by directly solving the infinite-dimensional primal-dual of continuous limit.

Let's think of optimization as a min-max game:
Normalized a, objective max_k b_k. At optimum, there is distribution λ over active lags such that gradient 2λ? P_k a is constant on support? Specifically subgradient: 0 ∈ {2∑ λ_k P_k a - μ 1} for a_i>0, and 2∑λ_k P_k a - μ 1 ≥0? For minimization with nonneg constraints? Because minimize f(a)=max b_k subject sum a=1, a≥0. KKT: There exists λ over active set with ∑λ=1 such that for all i with a_i>0: 2(λ * T? a)_i = μ; for zeros: gradient ≥ μ? (since minimize with a_i≥0 and sum, derivative of f - μ = λ?≥0?). The vector q_i=2∑λ_k (P_k a)_i is pressure. At optimum, q_i is constant on support, and high elsewhere. This matches coordinate pressure.

We can iteratively update λ and a:
- Given a, choose λ as softmax over b_k (active weights).
- Update a to minimize gradient pressure? For given λ, the objective to minimize is linear? f_λ(a)=∑λ b_k = a^T Q a (quadratic, not linear). But if we treat a fixed pressure q=2Q a, an Frank-Wolfe step to simplex point g minimizing q·g = min_i q_i gives descent for current gradient. This yields simple FW: g=unit vector at min pressure. That's coordinate descent (move all mass to min pressure point), too aggressive. Previous game_direction solved min_g max f*g (linear in g) with active λ as max over k; that's a better LP: minimize max_k f*g, where f current, not a^T Q a. This is linear in g and can be solved as two-player game. It produces a direction that simultaneously lowers all cross terms with current a. This is promising and can be improved by using second-order terms as well.

Maybe we can devise an algorithm to directly satisfy KKT:
1. Estimate active lags k where b_k near max.
2. Choose λ_weights to make pressure q_i as flat as possible over support. Solve for λ weights nonnegative sum1 minimizing variance of q_i (or maximizing min). Now, to improve over previous, include exact LP direction. Let's implement `exact_lp_direction(f, active_idx, max_k, time_limit)`:
```
def exact_lp_direction(f, active_idx, max_k=200, time_limit=5.0):
    if not SCIPY_OK or not highs_available(): return None
    n=f.size
    K=min(max_k, active_idx.size)
    if K<=0: return None
    active=active_idx[:K]
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        k=int(k)
        # j such that 0<=k-j<n => j in [max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)]
        lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
        if hi<lo: continue
        # coeff f[k-j] for j=lo..hi
        js=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
        coeffs=f[k-js]
        rows.extend(np.full(js.size,r)); cols.extend(js); vals.extend(coeffs)
    if not rows: return None
    A=scipy.sparse.coo_matrix((vals,(rr,cc)), shape=(K,n)).tocsr()
    ...
```
But Python `extend` in loop heavy. Use list comprehension chunks and np.concatenate.
```
row_parts=[]; col_parts=[]; val_parts=[]
...
row_parts.append(np.full(js.size, r, dtype=np.int32))
...
rows=np.concatenate(row_parts)
```
Memory okay.

The constraint matrix rows count actual `K_actual`. Use ragged shape.
Sign: `A_ub = scipy.sparse.hstack((A, -scipy.sparse.eye(K_actual, format='csr')), format='csr')`, b zeros.

`active_top(c, allowed, max_k)`:
```
if allowed is None: arr=c
else: arr=c[allowed]
if arr.size <= max_k: return allowed if allowed not None else np.arange(c.size)
pos=np.argpartition(arr, -max_k)[-max_k:]  # partial
# sort those by arr descending
top_local = pos[np.argsort(arr[pos])[::-1]]
if allowed is None: return top_local
return allowed[top_local]
```
Careful if duplicate; fine.

`cross_convolve_p_a`: Verify previous code implementation:
```
total=3*n-2
N=next_pow2(total)
A=fft.rfft(a,N)
Pr=fft.rfft(p[::-1],N)
conv=fft.irfft(A*Pr,N)[:total]
out=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return factor*out
```
Let's test small: a length2 [a0,a1], p length3 [p0,p1,p2]. Want grad wrt a0: 2? factor maybe1: ∑ p_k a_{k-0}: k=0 p0 a0, k=1 p1 a1, k=2 p2 a2 invalid? So grad0=p0a0+p1a1. grad1=p1a0+p2a1. - gv from gu excluding ref.

Return.

`coordinate_polish`:
```
def coordinate_polish(a0, budget, mask_idx, verbose=False):
   a=norm; c; M; best
   top_count=min(500, max(20, (2n-1)//10))
   while time_left>2 and stall<100:
      top=active_top(c, mask_idx, top_count)
      # weights softmax of top values: scale by 1/(range+eps)
      vals=c[top]; m=vals.max(); mn=vals.min(); if range=0 weight uniform else softmax(50*(vals-m)/range?)
      p=zeros(size); p[top]=weights
      pressure=cross_convolve_p_a(p,a)
      # candidates
      src_score=pressure * (a + 1e-9)
      dst_score= -pressure + 1e-3*a
      # take top 8 src, top 12 dst
      for s in src_list: if a[s]>1e-12:
         for d in dst_list: if d!=s:
            for frac in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9):
                tmove=a[s]*frac
                m_new=test_transfer(c,a,s,d,tmove,mask_idx)
                track best
      if no best: random 30
      apply best
```
`test_transfer(c,a,s,d,t,mask_idx)`:

cn=c.copy()
n=a.size
# linear
cn[d:d+n] += 2*t*a
cn[s:s+n] -= 2*t*a
cn[2*d] += t*t
cn[2*s] += t*t
cn[s+d] -= 2*t*t
# For d=s no; ensure indexes < size
cn[~finite]=0; max(np.maximum(cn,0), mask_idx)
```
If slice d:d+n may exceed? size=2n-1, d=n-1 -> d+n=2n-1 ok. s similarly. 2*s maybe if s=n-1 ->2n-2 ok. s+d max2n-2.

Shape functions:
```
def central_sequence(n):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(n)
    ratios=(2*np.arange(1,n)-1)/(2*np.arange(1,n))
    x=np.empty(n); x[0]=1; x[1:]=np.cumprod(ratios)
    return norm_sum1(x)
```

if both: x += 1.0/(n-1-i+0.5)**alpha
return norm_sum1(x)
```

In FW, exact LP every 6 when time_left>150 and active size maybe <=800. LP may dominate. Set exact_time=3.

`line_search_mix` 14 FFT? no, only array ops. Good.

`coordinate_polish`: For each round, candidates maybe 8*12*8=768 transfers. Each copy length20k and max => 768*20k=15M ops per round; if 50 rounds 750M, maybe slow in Python/numpy but vectorized C copy. Could reduce candidates: src 4, dst 6, fracs 5 =>120 per round. Use 4/6/5. Good.

`test_transfer` uses `cn=c.copy()` 20k; 120*20k=2.4M per round, okay.

Now, final code. Need be concise enough but can be long. Ensure final output only code block.

Let's write final code now.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing the evaluator's step
function objective.

The program:
- calibrates which convolution lags the evaluator rewards by comparing a small
  set of proxy masks against direct calls to evaluate_sequence,
- builds diverse initializations (parent interpolation, central-binomial
  profile, power-law profiles, smoothed random profiles),
- optimizes them with:
    * log-sum-exp smoothed active-set L-BFGS in softmax-coordinate variables,
    * Frank-Wolfe-style directions solved exactly with HiGHS when practical
      and approximately otherwise,
    * exact small-transfer coordinate polish,
- periodically retries slightly larger/shorter lengths,
- always returns the best valid sequence found before the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize
    import scipy.sparse
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OK = False

_HIGHS_OK = None


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def hihts_available():
    global _HIGHS_OK
    if _HIGHS_OK is None:
        ok = False
        if SCIPY_OK:
            try:
                res = scipy.optimize.linprog([-1.0], bounds=[(0.0, None)],
                                             method="HiGHS",
                                             options={"disp": False})
                ok = bool(res.success)
            except Exception:
                ok = False
        _HIGHS_OK = ok
    return _HIGHS_OK


def norm_sum1(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr[~np.isfinite(arr)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return arr / s


def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def fft_conv2(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size != n:
        if b.size > n:
            b = b[:n]
        else:
            nb = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            nb[:b.size] = b
            b = nb
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def proxy_max(c, idx):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    if idx is None:
        vals = c
    else:
        vals = c[idx]
    vals = vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(vals))


def active_top(c, allowed, max_k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    size = c.size
    if max_k <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if allowed is None:
        arr = c
        base = np.arange(size, dtype=int)
    else:
        allowed = np.asarray(allowed, dtype=int)
        arr = c[allowed]
        base = allowed
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    max_k = int(min(max_k, arr.size))
    if max_k >= arr.size:
        if allowed is None:
            return np.argsort(c)[::-1][:max_k]
        return base[np.argsort(arr)[::-1]][:max_k]
    part = np.argpartition(arr, -max_k)[-max_k:]
    order = np.argsort(arr[part])[::-1]
    chosen = part[order]
    if allowed is None:
        return chosen
    return base[chosen]


def make_top_mask(a, top_count, base_idx):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=bool), np.array([], dtype=int)
    if base_idx is None:
        allowed = np.arange(size, dtype=int)
    else:
        allowed = np.asarray(base_idx, dtype=int)
    c = fft_conv(a)
    top_count = int(top_count) if top_count is not None else allowed.size
    if top_count <= 0 or top_count >= allowed.size:
        m = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
        if allowed.size:
            m[allowed] = True
        return m, allowed
    top = active_top(c, allowed, top_count)
    m = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
    if top.size:
        m[top] = True
    return m, top


def cross_convolve_p_a(p, a, factor=1.0):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size_p = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != size_p:
        p = np.resize(p, size_p)
    total = 3 * n - 2
    N = next_pow2(total)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    Pr = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * Pr, N)[:total]
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return factor * out


def lse_a(a, beta, mask=None, reg_a=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    c = fft_conv(a)
    if mask is None:
        cv = c
    else:
        cv = c[mask]
    if cv.size == 0:
        f = 0.0
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        m = float(np.max(cv))
        shifted = beta * (cv - m)
        shifted = np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(shifted)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if se <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(se):
            e = np.zeros(cv.size, dtype=np.float64)
            e[0] = 1.0
            se = 1.0
        p = e / se

        pf = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        if mask is None:
            pf[:] = p
        else:
            pf[mask] = p

        grad = 2.0 * cross_convolve_p_a(pf, a, 1.0)
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
        f = m + math.log(se) / beta

    if reg_a > 0.0:
        f += 0.5 * reg_a * float(np.dot(a, a))
        grad = grad + reg_a * a
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
    return f, grad


def softmax_from_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    um = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - um)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def make_v(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0
    ref = int(np.argmax(a))
    a_ref = max(float(a[ref]), 1e-300)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) - math.log(a_ref)
    loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, -700.0)
    if ref == 0:
        v = loga[1:]
    elif ref == n - 1:
        v = loga[:-1]
    else:
        v = np.concatenate((loga[:ref], loga[ref + 1:]))
    v = np.clip(v, -700.0, 700.0)
    return v, ref


def from_v(v, ref):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size + 1
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = n - 1
    if ref > 0:
        u[:ref] = v[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        u[ref + 1:] = v[ref:]
    return softmax_from_u(u)


def objgrad_v(v, beta, mask=None, reg_u=0.0, ref=0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size + 1
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = n - 1
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if ref > 0:
        u[:ref] = v[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        u[ref + 1:] = v[ref:]
    a = softmax_from_u(u)
    f, ga = lse_a(a, beta, mask, 0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
        ga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mean = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gu = a * (ga - mean)
    gv = np.empty(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if ref > 0:
        gv[:ref] = gu[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        gv[ref:] = gu[ref + 1:]
    if reg_u > 0.0:
        f += 0.5 * reg_u * float(np.dot(v, v))
        gv = gv + reg_u * v
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gv)):
        gv = np.zeros(v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return f, gv


def estimate_lbfegs_cost(a, mask=None, beta=30.0):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return 0.10
    try:
        v, ref = make_v(a)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        objgrad_v(v, beta, mask, 0.0, ref)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        if dt <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(dt):
            dt = 0.05
        return float(dt)
    except Exception:
        return 0.10


def projected_gradient_fallback(a0, beta, mask, steps, lr0=0.10, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or steps <= 0:
        return a
    for it in range(steps):
        try:
            _, ga = lse_a(a, beta, mask, 0.0)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(ga)):
                break
            grad_t = ga - np.mean(ga)
            lr = lr0 / (1.0 + 0.05 * it)
            b = a - lr * grad_t
            b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
            np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s < 1e-15:
                b = a.copy()
            else:
                b = b / s
            if np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
                a = b
        except Exception:
            break
    if verbose:
        print("    PG fallback steps=" + str(steps))
    return a


def run_lbfegs(a0, beta, mask, maxfun=300, reg_u=1e-12, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, 0
    v0, ref = make_v(a)
    if not SCIPY_OK or maxfun <= 0:
        b = projected_gradient_fallback(v0.size and a, beta, mask, max(10, maxfun // 20), verbose=verbose)
        return b, 0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    options = {
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxiter": int(maxfun + 50),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "disp": False,
        "maxls": 50
    }
    res = None
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
            objgrad_v,
            v0,
            args=(beta, mask, reg_u, ref),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options=options
        )
    except Exception as e:
        print("    L-BFGS exception: " + repr(e))
        res = None

    a_out = a.copy()
    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if vx.size == v0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
            t = from_v(vx, ref)
            t = norm_sum1(t)
            a_out = t
    else:
        a_out = projected_gradient_fallback(a, beta, mask, max(10, maxfun // 20), verbose=False)

    if verbose:
        print("    L-BFGS beta=" + repr(beta) +
              " n=" + str(n) +
              " maxfun<=" + str(int(maxfun)) +
              " time=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t0) +
              " success=" + str(getattr(res, "success", False)))
    return a_out, 1


def lbfegs_phase(a_init, base_idx, budget, cost_eval, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a_init)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, base_idx)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    current_a = a.copy()

    schedules = [
        (8.0, None),
        (20.0, None),
        (50.0, 0.12),
        (100.0, 0.07),
        (200.0, 0.04),
        (400.0, 0.025),
        (700.0, 0.015),
        (1200.0, 0.010)
    ]

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(1, int(budget / max(2.5 * cost_eval, 0.03)))
    per_run = int(max(50, min(500, max(1, remaining // max(1, len(schedules))))))

    for beta, frac in schedules:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget - 12:
            break
        if frac is None:
            msk, idx = (None, base_idx)
        else:
            top_count = int(min(size, max(20, int(size * frac))))
            msk, idx = make_top_mask(current_a, top_count, base_idx)
        a_new, _ = run_lbfegs(current_a, beta, msk, maxfun=per_run, verbose=(verbose and True))
        c_new = fft_conv(a_new)
        m_new = proxy_max(c_new, base_idx)
        if math.isfinite(m_new):
            if m_new <= best_M * (1.0 + 1e-5) + 1e-12:
                current_a = a_new
                if m_new < M:
                    c = c_new
                    M = m_new
            if m_new < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = m_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
    return best_a


def line_search_mix(a, g, c, cg, fg, idx, current_M):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    alphas = [0.01, 0.02, 0.04, 0.07, 0.11, 0.17, 0.25, 0.35, 0.47, 0.60, 0.74, 0.87, 0.96, 1.0]
    best_alpha = 0.0
    best_m = current_M
    best_c = c
    for alpha in alphas:
        alpha = float(alpha)
        if alpha <= 0.0 or alpha > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            continue
        alpha = min(1.0, alpha)
        one = 1.0 - alpha
        val = (one * one) * c + (2.0 * alpha * one) * fg + (alpha * alpha) * cg
        val = np.where(np.isfinite(val) & (val > 0.0), val, 0.0)
        m = proxy_max(val, idx)
        if math.isfinite(m) and m < best_m - 1e-16:
            best_m = m
            best_alpha = alpha
            best_c = val
    if best_alpha <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), best_m, 0.0, best_c
    new_a = (1.0 - best_alpha) * a + best_alpha * g
    new_a = np.where(np.isfinite(new_a), new_a, 0.0)
    np.maximum(new_a, 0.0, out=new_a)
    s = float(new_a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return a.copy(), best_m, 0.0, best_c
    new_a = new_a / s
    return new_a, best_m, best_alpha, best_c


def game_direction(f, active_idx, mw_iters=14, seed=0, init_logits=None):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    n = f.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int)
    K = active_idx.size
    if K <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if init_logits is None:
        logits = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        logits = np.asarray(init_logits, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if logits.size != K:
            base = logits[:min(K, logits.size)] if logits.size > 0 else np.zeros(1)
            while base.size < K:
                base = np.concatenate((base, base))
            logits = base[:K]
        logits = np.where(np.isfinite(logits), logits, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(logits))
        if math.isfinite(mx):
            logits = logits - mx
    logits += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, K)

    def normalize_logits(loc):
        loc = np.where(np.isfinite(loc), loc, -1000.0)
        loc = loc - float(np.max(loc))
        w = np.exp(loc)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / s
        return w

    lambda_w = normalize_logits(logits)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(mw_iters))):
        pf = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        pf[active_idx] = lambda_w
        pressure = cross_convolve_p_a(pf, f, 1.0)
        pressure = np.where(np.isfinite(pressure) & (pressure > 0.0), pressure, 0.0)
        pmx = float(np.max(pressure))
        pmn = float(np.min(pressure))
        ptp = pmx - pmn
        if ptp <= 1e-30:
            g = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            scaled = (pressure - pmn) / ptp
            w = np.exp(-9.0 * scaled)
            w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
            w += 1e-12
            g = w / float(w.sum())
            g = 0.98 * g + 0.02 * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
            g = g / float(g.sum())

        h = fft_conv2(f, g)
        ha = h[active_idx]
        ha = np.where(np.isfinite(ha), ha, 0.0)
        hmac_ = float(np.max(ha))
        hmn = float(np.min(ha))
        hptp = hmac_ - hmn
        if hptp > 1e-30:
            eta = 8.0 / hptp
            logits += eta * (ha - hmac_)
        logits = np.where(np.isfinite(logits), logits, 0.0)
        logits = np.clip(logits, -700.0, 700.0)
        lambda_w = normalize_logits(logits)
    return g


def exact_lp_direction(f, active_idx, max_k=180, time_limit=4.0):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return None
    if not hihts_available():
        return None
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    n = f.size
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int)
    if active_idx.size == 0:
        return None
    max_k = int(min(max_k, active_idx.size))
    active = active_idx[:max_k]

    row_parts = []
    col_parts = []
    val_parts = []
    actual_rows = 0
    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        cols = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int32)
        coeffs = f[int(k) - cols].astype(np.float64)
        row_parts.append(np.full(cols.size, actual_rows, dtype=np.int32))
        col_parts.append(cols)
        val_parts.append(coeffs)
        actual_rows += 1

    if actual_rows == 0:
        return None
    rows = np.concatenate(row_parts)
    cols = np.concatenate(col_parts)
    vals = np.concatenate(val_parts)

    try:
        A = scipy.sparse.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(actual_rows, n))
    except Exception:
        return None

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0
    neg_t = scipy.sparse.csc_matrix(-np.ones((actual_rows, 1)), dtype=np.float64)
    A_ub = scipy.sparse.hstack((A, neg_t), format="csr")
    A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(actual_rows, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(None, None)]

    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="HiGHS",
            options={"disp": False, "time_limit": float(time_limit)}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs < 1e-15:
        return None
    return g / gs


def frank_wolfe_search(a0, budget, idx, active_max=1600, game_iters=12,
                       exact_every=6, exact_time=3.0, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, idx)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget - 8 and no_improve < 100:
        it += 1
        time_left = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if time_left < 20:
            break
        active = active_top(c, idx, int(active_max))
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        g = None
        if hihts_available() and (it % exact_every == 0) and time_left > 90:
            g = exact_lp_direction(a, active, max_k=160, time_limit=max(1.0, min(exact_time, time_left / 4.0)))

        if g is None:
            init_log = None
            if active.size > 0:
                av = c[active]
                av = np.where(np.isfinite(av) & (av > 0.0), av, 1e-300)
                init_log = np.log(av)
                init_log -= float(np.max(init_log))
            g = game_direction(a, active, mw_iters=game_iters, seed=101 + it, init_logits=init_log)

        if g is None:
            break

        cg = fft_conv(g)
        fg = fft_conv2(a, g)
        new_a, new_M, alpha, new_c = line_search_mix(a, g, c, cg, fg, idx, M)
        if new_M < M - 1e-15 and alpha > 0.0:
            a = new_a
            c = new_c
            M = new_M
            no_improve = 0
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
                if verbose:
                    print("    FW t=" + str(it) +
                          " proxy_score=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_M) +
                          " alpha=" + repr(alpha) +
                          " active=" + str(active.size))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 12 == 0:
                rng = np.random.default_rng(999 + it)
                pert = best_a * rng.uniform(0.92, 1.08, n)
                pert += best_a * rng.normal(0.0, 0.012, n)
                pert = np.where(np.isfinite(pert), pert, 0.0)
                np.maximum(pert, 0.0, out=pert)
                ps = float(pert.sum())
                if ps > 1e-15:
                    pert = pert / ps
                else:
                    pert = best_a.copy()
                cp = fft_conv(pert)
                Mp = proxy_max(cp, idx)
                if math.isfinite(Mp):
                    a = pert
                    c = cp
                    M = Mp
                no_improve = 0
    return best_a


def test_transfer(c, a, s, d, tmove, idx):
    cn = c.copy()
    n = a.size
    cn[d:d + n] += 2.0 * tmove * a
    cn[s:s + n] -= 2.0 * tmove * a
    if 2 * d < cn.size:
        cn[2 * d] += tmove * tmove
    if 2 * s < cn.size:
        cn[2 * s] += tmove * tmove
    if s + d < cn.size:
        cn[s + d] -= 2.0 * tmove * tmove
    cn = np.where(np.isfinite(cn) & (cn > 0.0), cn, 0.0)
    return proxy_max(cn, idx)


def top_score_indices(score, k):
    score = np.asarray(score, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(min(max(1, k), score.size))
    if k >= score.size:
        return np.argsort(score)[::-1][:k]
    part = np.argpartition(score, -k)[-k:]
    part = part[np.argsort(score[part])[::-1]]
    return part


def coordinate_polish(a0, budget, idx, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, idx)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    stall = 0
    rnd = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    top_count = int(min(450, max(24, (2 * n - 1) // 10)))
    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.45, 0.65, 0.85)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget - 3 and stall < 80:
        rnd += 1
        if n <= 1:
            break
        top = active_top(c, idx, top_count)
        if top.size == 0:
            break
        p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p[top] = 1.0 / float(top.size)
        pressure = cross_convolve_p_a(p, a, 1.0)
        pressure = np.where(np.isfinite(pressure), pressure, 0.0)

        src = top_score_indices(pressure * (a + 1e-12), min(6, n))
        dst = top_score_indices(-pressure + 1e-4 * a, min(10, n))

        best_m = M
        best_s = -1
        best_d = -1
        best_t = 0.0

        for sr in src:
            s = int(sr)
            if a[s] < 1e-13:
                continue
            for dr in dst:
                d = int(dr)
                if d == s:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    tmove = a[s] * frac
                    tmove = min(tmove, a[s] * 0.97)
                    if tmove <= 1e-16:
                        continue
                    m_new = test_transfer(c, a, s, d, tmove, idx)
                    if math.isfinite(m_new) and m_new < best_m - 1e-16:
                        best_m = m_new
                        best_s = s
                        best_d = d
                        best_t = tmove

        if best_s < 0:
            for _ in range(30):
                s = int(rng.integers(n))
                d = int(rng.integers(n))
                if s == d or a[s] < 1e-13:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.18))
                tmove = min(a[s] * frac, a[s] * 0.5)
                if tmove <= 1e-16:
                    continue
                m_new = test_transfer(c, a, s, d, tmove, idx)
                if math.isfinite(m_new) and m_new < best_m - 1e-16:
                    best_m = m_new
                    best_s = s
                    best_d = d
                    best_t = tmove

        if best_s >= 0:
            a[best_s] -= best_t
            a[best_d] += best_t
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
            np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss < 1e-15:
                break
            a = a / ss
            c = fft_conv(a)
            M = proxy_max(c, idx)
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and (rnd <= 2 or rnd % 40 == 0):
                print("    Coord rnd=" + str(rnd) +
                      " proxy_score=" + repr(2.0 * n * best_M) +
                      " move=" + str(best_s) + "->" + str(best_d))
        else:
            stall += 1
    return best_a


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if a.size == new_n:
        return norm_sum1(a)
    if a.size == 1:
        return norm_sum1(np.full(new_n, float(a[0])))
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
    return norm_sum1(y)


def central_sequence(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    ratios = (2.0 * np.arange(1, n) - 1.0) / (2.0 * np.arange(1, n))
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x[0] = 1.0
    x[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x > 0.0), x, 0.0)
    return norm_sum1(x)


def power_sequence(n, alpha=0.5, both=False):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 1.0 / np.power(i + 0.5, alpha)
    if both:
        x += 1.0 / np.power(n - 1.0 - i + 0.5, alpha)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x) & (x > 0.0), x, 0.0)
    return norm_sum1(x)


def block_sequence(n):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    L = max(2, int(n // 4))
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:L] = 1.0
    start = min(n - L, 2 * L)
    a[start:start + L] = 1.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        return central_sequence(n)
    return a / s


def smooth_random_sequence(n, seed=1):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.65, n))
    N = next_pow2(n)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(N) * 2.0
    smooth = np.exp(-freq * 3.0)
    xs = np.fft.irfft(X * smooth, N)[:n]
    y = np.abs(x) + np.abs(xs) + 0.05
    return norm_sum1(y)


def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return math.inf
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0, out=arr)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return math.inf
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        if val is None:
            return math.inf
        val = float(val)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return math.inf
        return val
    except Exception:
        return math.inf


def proxy_sequence_score(a, idx):
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    c = fft_conv(a)
    m = proxy_max(c, idx)
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def get_mask_candidates(n):
    size = 2 * n - 1
    cands = []
    cands.append(("full", None))

    m = np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
    m[n - 1] = False
    cands.append(("no_center", np.nonzero(m)[0]))

    m2 = m.copy()
    m2[0] = False
    m2[-1] = False
    cands.append(("no_center_endpoints", np.nonzero(m2)[0]))

    m3 = np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
    m3[0] = False
    m3[-1] = False
    cands.append(("no_endpoints", np.nonzero(m3)[0]))

    m4 = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
    m4[:max(1, n)] = True
    cands.append(("left", np.nonzero(m4)[0]))

    m5 = np.zeros(size, dtype=bool)
    m5[n - 1:] = True
    cands.append(("right", np.nonzero(m5)[0]))

    near = max(1, n // 100)
    m6 = np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
    lo = max(0, n - 1 - near)
    hi = min(size, n + near)
    m6[lo:hi] = False
    cands.append(("no_center_band", np.nonzero(m6)[0]))

    return cands


def calibrate_mask(parent, budget=25.0, eval_len=2048):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    cands = get_mask_candidates(eval_len)
    seqs = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        seqs.append(interpolate_sequence(parent, eval_len))
    seqs.append(central_sequence(eval_len))
    seqs.append(power_sequence(eval_len, 0.5))
    seqs.append(smooth_random_sequence(eval_len, 7))

    errs = {name: 0.0 for name, _ in cands}
    counts = {name: 0 for name, _ in cands}
    worst_bounded = {name: math.inf for name, _ in cands}

    for a in seqs:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(5.0, budget - 4.0):
            break
        actual = safe_eval(a)
        if not math.isfinite(actual):
            continue
        c = fft_conv(a)
        for name, idx in cands:
            m = proxy_max(c, idx)
            pred = 2.0 * a.size * m
            if math.isfinite(pred):
                e = abs(pred - actual)
                errs[name] += e
                counts[name] += 1
                if counts[name] == 1:
                    worst_bounded[name] = max(0.05, 0.08 * actual + 1e-6)

    best_name = "full"
    best_val = math.inf
    for name, _ in cands:
        cnt = max(1, counts[name])
        val = errs[name] / cnt
        if val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_name = name
    for name, idx in cands:
        if name == "full":
            return None, None
        if name == best_name:
            return idx, idx
    return None, None


def maybe_candidate(a, idx, best_seq, best_score, best_proxy):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score, best_proxy
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score, best_proxy
    proxy = proxy_sequence_score(a, idx)
    if not math.isfinite(proxy):
        return best_seq, best_score, best_proxy
    if proxy < best_proxy - 1e-13 or best_seq is None:
        actual = safe_eval(a)
        if math.isfinite(actual) and (actual < best_score - 1e-15 or best_seq is None):
            return a.copy(), float(actual), float(proxy)
    return best_seq, best_score, best_proxy


def optimize_sequence(a0, budget, mask, idx, cost_eval, t_global, label=""):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_conv(a)
    M = proxy_max(c, idx)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    best_actual = math.inf
    print("  optimize " + label + " n=" + str(n) + " start_proxy=" + repr(2.0 * n * M))

    if cost_eval <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(cost_eval):
        cost_eval = estimate_lbfegs_cost(a, mask, 25.0)

    def integrate_candidate(x, tag):
        nonlocal best_a, best_M, best_actual
        if x is None:
            return
        x = norm_sum1(x)
        px = proxy_sequence_score(x, idx)
        if math.isfinite(px):
            if px < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = px
                best_a = x.copy()
            actual = safe_eval(x)
            if math.isfinite(actual) and actual < best_actual:
                best_actual = actual
                print("  NEW " + label + " " + tag + " actual=" + repr(actual) +
                      " proxy=" + repr(px) + " n=" + str(n) +
                      " t=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t_global))

    if budget > 120 and SCIPY_OK:
        lb_budget = min(budget * 0.48, max(60.0, budget - 320.0))
        integrate_candidate(lbfegs_phase(a, idx, lb_budget, cost_eval, verbose=False), "lbfegs")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    if budget - elapsed > 90:
        fw_budget = min((budget - elapsed) * 0.34, 260.0)
        integrate_candidate(
            frank_wolfe_search(best_a, fw_budget, idx,
                               active_max=min(1800, max(80, max(1, (2 * n - 1) // 8))),
                               game_iters=11, exact_every=7, exact_time=2.5, verbose=False),
            "fw"
        )

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    if budget - elapsed > 60:
        cp_budget = min((budget - elapsed) * 0.45, 220.0)
        integrate_candidate(coordinate_polish(best_a, cp_budget, idx, verbose=False), "coord")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
    if budget - elapsed > 45 and SCIPY_OK:
        top_frac = 0.02
        msk, midx = make_top_mask(best_a, int(min(2 * n - 1, max(30, (2 * n - 1) * top_frac))), idx)
        per = int(max(50, min(240, int((budget - elapsed) * 0.45 / max(3.0 * cost_eval, 0.03)))))
        integrate_candidate(run_lbfegs(best_a, 280.0, msk, maxfun=per, verbose=False)[0], "final_lb")

    print("  finished " + label + " n=" + str(n) +
          " final_proxy=" + repr(2.0 * best_a.size * proxy_sequence_score(best_a, idx) / max(1.0, best_a.size)) +
          " actual=" + repr(best_actual))
    return best_a, best_actual


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(60.0, min(1725.0, float(budget_s) - 75.0))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1725.0
    print("start budget=" + repr(total_budget))

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0, out=p)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.ravel()
        if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-10:
            parent = norm_sum1(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = central_sequence(6000)
    print("parent n=" + str(parent.size) + " raw_parent_score=" + repr(safe_eval(parent)))

    mask_idx, _ = calibrate_mask(parent, budget=20.0, eval_len=2048)
    if mask_idx is None:
        print("calibration: full mask")
    else:
        print("calibration: custom mask, active lags=" + str(mask_idx.size))

    MAX_N = 11500 if total_budget > 1450 else 9500
    base_n = int(max(2500, min(MAX_N, parent.size)))
    lengths = []
    for x in [3000, 4000, 5000, 6000, base_n,
              max(2500, int(base_n * 1.08)),
              max(2500, int(base_n * 1.18)),
              8000, 9500, MAX_N]:
        x = int(max(2000, min(MAX_N, x)))
        if x not in lengths:
            lengths.append(x)
    lengths.sort()

    pool = []
    for L in lengths:
        if parent.size == L:
            a = norm_sum1(parent)
        else:
            a = interpolate_sequence(parent, L)
        pool.append(a)
        if L in (base_n, lengths[-1]):
            pool.append(central_sequence(L))
            pool.append(power_sequence(L, 0.45))
            pool.append(power_sequence(L, 0.70, both=True))
            pool.append(block_sequence(L))
            pool.append(smooth_random_sequence(L, 11))

    scored_pool = []
    for a in pool:
        px = proxy_sequence_score(a, mask_idx)
        if math.isfinite(px):
            scored_pool.append((px, a))
    scored_pool.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    best_seq = None
    best_score = math.inf
    best_proxy = math.inf

    seen_lengths = set()
    considered = 0
    for px, a in scored_pool:
        if considered >= 12:
            break
        if a.size in seen_lengths and px > best_proxy * 1.02:
            continue
        seen_lengths.add(a.size)
        considered += 1
        best_seq, best_score, best_proxy = maybe_candidate(a, mask_idx, best_seq, best_score, best_proxy)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = norm_sum1(parent)
        best_score = safe_eval(best_seq)
        best_proxy = proxy_sequence_score(best_seq, mask_idx)
        if not math.isfinite(best_proxy):
            best_proxy = 1e100
    print("initial best: n=" + str(best_seq.size) + " actual=" + repr(best_score) +
          " proxy=" + repr(best_proxy))

    # Select up to two starting sequences for deeper optimization.
    starts = [(best_proxy, best_seq.copy())]
    for px, a in scored_pool:
        if a.size == best_seq.size and np.allclose(a, best_seq, rtol=1e-4, atol=1e-8):
            continue
        starts.append((px, a))
        if len(starts) >= 3:
            break

    remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start) - 180.0
    if remaining > 80:
        if len(starts) == 1:
            budgets = [remaining]
        elif len(starts) == 2:
            budgets = [remaining * 0.62, remaining * 0.38]
        else:
            budgets = [remaining * 0.52, remaining * 0.28, remaining * 0.20]

        for i, (px, st) in enumerate(starts[:len(budgets)]):
            el = time.monotonic() - t_start
            rb = total_budget - el - 180.0
            if rb < 70:
                break
            b = min(budgets[i], rb)
            cost = estimate_lbfegs_cost(st, mask_idx, 25.0)
            cand, actual = optimize_sequence(st, b, mask_idx, mask_idx, cost, t_start, label="start%d" % i)
            if actual is not None and math.isfinite(actual) and actual < best_score:
                best_score = float(actual)
                best_seq = norm_sum1(cand)
                best_proxy = proxy_sequence_score(best_seq, mask_idx)
            elif cand is not None:
                p2 = proxy_sequence_score(cand, mask_idx)
                if math.isfinite(p2) and p2 < best_proxy:
                    # Actual may be bad but keep as safer fallback? Prefer evaluated.
                    act2 = safe_eval(cand)
                    if math.isfinite(act2) and act2 < best_score:
                        best_score = act2
                        best_seq = norm_sum1(cand)
                        best_proxy = p2

    # Length probing / final expansion.
    el = time.monotonic() - t_start
    if best_seq is not None and total_budget - el > 140 and best_seq.size < MAX_N:
        targets = []
        for x in [int(best_seq.size * 1.12), min(MAX_N, int(best_seq.size * 1.22)), int(best_seq.size + 700)]:
            x = int(max(2500, min(MAX_N, x)))
            if x > best_seq.size and x not in targets:
                targets.append(x)
        for tt in targets[:2]:
            el = time.monotonic() - t_start
            if total_budget - el < 70:
                break
            a0 = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, tt)
            cost = estimate_lbfegs_cost(a0, mask_idx, 30.0)
            b = min((total_budget - el) * 0.70, 260.0)
            cand, actual = optimize_sequence(a0, b, mask_idx, mask_idx, cost, t_start, label="len%d" % tt)
            if actual is not None and math.isfinite(actual) and actual < best_score:
                best_score = float(actual)
                best_seq = norm_sum1(cand)
                best_proxy = proxy_sequence_score(best_seq, mask_idx)

    # Final polish on best.
    el = time.monotonic() - t_start
    if best_seq is not None and total_budget - el > 55:
        cand, actual = optimize_sequence(best_seq, min(240.0, total_budget - el - 35.0),
                                         mask_idx, mask_idx,
                                         estimate_lbfegs_cost(best_seq, mask_idx, 60.0),
                                         t_start, label="final")
        if actual is not None and math.isfinite(actual) and actual < best_score:
            best_score = float(actual)
            best_seq = norm_sum1(cand)
            best_proxy = proxy_sequence_score(best_seq, mask_idx)

    if best_seq is None or not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_seq = norm_sum1(parent)
        best_score = safe_eval(best_seq)

    final = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(final.sum())
    if final.size == 0 or ss < 0.01:
        final = central_sequence(max(5000, parent.size if parent is not None else 5000))
    else:
        final = final / ss

    final_val = safe_eval(final)
    if not math.isfinite(final_val):
        final = norm_sum1(parent)
        final_val = safe_eval(final)
    print("FINAL: score=" + repr(final_val) + " n=" + str(final.size) +
          " elapsed=" + repr(time.monotonic() - t_start))
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
